defStyleAttr) {\n        super(context, attrs, defStyleAttr);\n    }\n\n    public void setOnTransformationMethodChangedListener(@Nullable OnTransformationMethodChangedListener listener) {\n        this.transformationMethodChangedListener = listener;\n    }\n\n    @Override\n    public void setTransformationMethod(@Nullable TransformationMethod method) {\n        super.setTransformationMethod(method);\n\n        // This might be called from the constructor of the base class, before our own fields\n        // have been initialized, so the listener needs to be null-checked.\n        if (transformationMethodChangedListener != null)\n            transformationMethodChangedListener.onTransformationMethodChanged(getTransformationMethod());\n    }\n}\n'})], AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The fix is complete and the project builds. Here\'s a summary of the diagnosis and patch.\n\n## Diagnosis\n\nThe "show password" affordance is the Material `TextInputLayout`\'s password toggle (`app:passwordToggleEnabled="true"`), which is only attached to the *password* field:\n\n- `component_intro_authentication.xml` → `introPasswordLayout`/`introPasswordEdit` (+ plain `introPasswordConfirm` EditText)\n- `component_authentication.xml`, `component_password.xml`, `dialog_password_entry.xml` → same pattern\n\nMaterial\'s toggle simply swaps the `TransformationMethod` of *its own* `EditText`. The confirmation field is a separate, plain `EditText` outside the layout, so nothing ever changed its transformation method — hence it stayed masked while the password field was revealed.\n\nThe toggle exposes no state-change callback (`TextInputLayout` in material 1.1.0 only has `addOnEndIconChangedListener`, for icon *mode*), and `TextView.setTransformationMethod()` is `final`, so it can\'t be overridden. However, `setTransformationMethod()` internally re-sets the text, which dispatches to `TextWatcher`s — the same mechanism Material\'s own `PasswordToggleEndIconDelegate` uses to keep its eye icon in sync. That gives a reliable hook.\n\n## Patch\n\n**`Utilities/UIHelper.java`** — new reusable helper that mirrors the password field\'s text visibility onto any number of linked fields:\n\n```java\npublic static void linkTextVisibility(final EditText passwordInput, final EditText... linkedInputs) {\n    syncTextVisibility(passwordInput, linkedInputs);\n    passwordInput.addTextChangedListener(new TextWatcher() {\n        @Override public void beforeTextChanged(CharSequence s, int start, int count, int after) {\n            syncTextVisibility(passwordInput, linkedInputs);\n        }\n        ...\n    });\n}\n\nprivate static void syncTextVisibility(EditText source, EditText... inputs) {\n    boolean hidden = isTextHidden(source);\n    for (EditText input : inputs) {\n        if (input == null || isTextHidden(input) == hidden) continue;   // no needless churn\n        int selectionStart = input.getSelectionStart();                 // preserve cursor\n        int selectionEnd = input.getSelectionEnd();\n        input.setTransformationMethod(hidden ? PasswordTransformationMethod.getInstance() : null);\n        if (selectionStart >= 0 && selectionEnd >= 0) input.setSelection(selectionStart, selectionEnd);\n    }\n}\n\nprivate static boolean isTextHidden(EditText input) {\n    return input.getTransformationMethod() instanceof PasswordTransformationMethod;\n}\n```\n\n**Call sites wired up** (one line each, right after the views are resolved):\n- `Activities/IntroScreenActivity.java` (`AuthenticationFragment`) — the case from the bug report (first-time setup, Password *and* PIN)\n- `Preferences/CredentialsPreference.java` — settings → authentication method\n- `Preferences/PasswordEncryptedPreference.java` — settings → password/PIN\n- `Dialogs/PasswordEntryDialog.java` (UPDATE mode) — backup password update, same defect\n\n## Notes\n\n- No layout or public-API changes: IDs, view classes and styling are untouched, so existing behavior/tests are preserved.\n- Sync is applied once at setup (initial state) and on every transformation change, and it\'s idempotent: it skips fields whose visibility already matches, so typing doesn\'t cause repeated work or cursor jumps.\n- Switching between Password/PIN (which calls `setInputType`) also re-masks both fields consistently, matching Material\'s icon state.\n- `./gradlew clean assembleDebug` → **BUILD SUCCESSFUL** (both `fdroid` and `play` flavors).')], model='claude-opus-5', parent_tool_use_id=None, error=None, usage={'input_tokens': 2, 'cache_creation_input_tokens': 272, 'cache_read_input_tokens': 71732, 'cache_creation': {'ephemeral_5m_input_tokens': 272, 'ephemeral_1h_input_tokens': 0}, 'output_tokens': 3, 'service_tier': 'standard', 'inference_geo': 'global'}, message_id='msg_011CfYHfrMYLv1auaHwDx5eW', stop_reason=None, session_id='61708896-1dff-4426-98ed-1da7de5c2a80', uuid='46a32733-bd15-4c6a-96b2-4531329d4ac6')
